# Page packets — Prosperity

47 pages, 6.625 x 10.25 inches. Made by the writers' room; every packet below is complete on its own.

## How to use these packets

1. **Start with the sheets** below: the character sheet, then the location sheet. Paste each,
   keep the results, and attach them as reference images with every page that names them
   (each page packet says which). Approve one page as your style page and attach that too.
   For a long book, train a LoRA on the approved sheets and pages; references alone drift.
2. **One page at a time.** Paste everything under a page's heading, nothing else. Keep the
   same model and settings for the whole book so the style and the characters stay put.
3. **No text on the pages.** The words are added afterwards, in the room, as a layer over
   your art - so a beautiful model that cannot spell is fine. If the model draws any letters,
   regenerate; do not keep a page with text on it.
4. **Check the page** against its checklist before you keep it. One thing wrong: regenerate
   with the same packet, and only then adjust wording.
5. **Upload each kept page** in the room (Production, Lettering tab) and run the lettering:
   the room draws every balloon and caption where the layout put them, and you download the
   finished, lettered page.

## Rules for every page

- Never render text of any kind: no balloons, captions, sound effects, page numbers,
  titles, chapter names, signatures, watermarks, nameplates or labels.
- Keep the areas each panel lists as clear: uncluttered art (sky, wall, shadow) where a
  balloon will sit, never a face or the thing the panel is about.
- Draw every character exactly as described, every time. The description wins over the
  previous page's picture.
- Panel count, panel shape and reading order are fixed by the map. Do not add, merge or
  reshape panels.

## Character sheet — draw this first

_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one character reference sheet: every character below standing full length in a row, front view, neutral pose, even daylight, plain light background, in the book's style. Same scale for everyone so heights compare. No text, names, labels or captions anywhere on the sheet.

**Style:**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

A single vertical infrastructure spine runs through the mountain, carrying: The visual contrast between the cities is strong but continuous.
- **KEEL** — Keel is built into and around an exhausted open-pit lithium mine. It contains: Keel's visual palette is rust, charcoal and oxidized orange. Dust hangs in the air. - **OASIS** — Oasis lies above Keel and Halyard, partly beyond the cloud layer. It contains: Oasis uses pearl white, silver, cool blue and controlled green. ### Panel 1 — ESTABLISHING, HIGH ANGLE

**Scene:** Night over restored Lake Keel in 2099: terraces, gardens and workshops ring the water while a tanker barge waits offshore. Two drones lower siphons into the lake; keep the upper sky clear.

**Keep these areas clear — the lettering is added afterwards as a separate layer, so draw no words here, just uncluttered art with room for a balloon:**

1. top left — space for a speech balloon of about 6 words

## Page 2 of 64 — Prosperity

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. ## Page 5 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **NERA** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **VOSS** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. ## Page 7 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **GRANDDAUGHTER** — **Causality** - **Public role:** Small repair robot dog living with older Alex in the 2099 prologue. - **Immediate want:** Investigate and interfere with the drones stealing lake water. - **Deeper need / contradiction:** He is built from scraps but repeatedly acts as if usefulness is worth the risk of coming apart. - **Leverage / capability:** Can separate his head, legs and body segments to enter gaps, scout and manipulate machinery. - **Vulnerability:** Magnetic separation, missing parts, dark
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

```text
## Page 7 (right) — The granddaughter asks what Alex’s past has to do with the lake.

**Panel 1.** Alex fits TJ’s body together. One leg is missing.

GRANDDAUGHTER: How did you get this lake?

ALEX: I didn’t.

**Panel 2.** She turns the damaged component over. A small maker’s mark is stamped into its casing.

GRANDDAUGHTER: That mark’s on the old pumps.

ALEX: It was there before me.

**Panel 3.** Across the lake, the tanker becomes a dark speck.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Do you know who sent them?

Alex closes his hand around the component.

ALEX: Not yet.

**Panel 4.** The component’s amber indicator blinks beside an open public water terminal.

PAGE CHECK: The prologue’s question belongs to Alex and the granddaughter, not an abstract thesis.

---
```

```text
## Page 9 (right) — The granddaughter finds a personal clue in the evidence.

**Panel 1.** The granddaughter studies the stamped mark on the component, then looks at Alex’s old steel watch.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Same mark.

**Panel 2.** Alex covers the watch with his thumb.

ALEX: Your eyes work.

**Panel 3.** TJ leans crookedly against Alex’s boot. The missing leg lies on the dock.

GRANDDAUGHTER: You’re going to tell me someday.

ALEX: Someday’s crowded.

**Panel 4.** She pockets the component.

GRANDDAUGHTER: I’ll keep asking.

PAGE CHECK: The future frame now ends on a relationship and a withheld answer.
```

## Page 10 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 1 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. - **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. ## Page 13 (right) — Ada gives Alex part of her ration and challenges his plan to leave.

```text
ALEX: The balance disagrees.

**Panel 4.** Ada studies him.

ADA: I’m your friend, Alex. Not your exit plan.

PAGE CHECK: Their friendship is explicit and non-romantic.
```

```text
## Page 14 (left) — Adrian’s absence remains a disputed memory.

**Panel 1.** Alex shows Ada an old photograph: Grandmother, Adrian with his face folded away and two-year-old Alex.

ALEX: I was two.

ADA: You remember nothing?

ALEX: The watch.

**Panel 2.** Close on the worn steel watch.

ADA: Keel says he gambled.

**Panel 3.** Ada turns the photograph toward the light.

ADA: Grandmother says he worked.

Alex says nothing.

**Panel 4.** Wind lifts dust across the pit rim.

ADA: What are you looking for?

ALEX: The part that costs.

PAGE CHECK: Adrian is neither cleared nor condemned.

---
```

## Page 15 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **PILAR** — Pilar Veyra is a sturdy Keel clinic worker in her forties, with medium-brown skin, dark hair braided at the nape, dark attentive eyes and a broad, reassuring face. She wears a faded teal clinic jacket over a charcoal shirt, durable trousers, flat shoes and a paper medication ledger secured in a clear sleeve; her silhouette is practical and steady. Her signature detail is a pencil tucked behind her right ear and a handwritten dose grid on her wrist. **Voice** — Calm, plain and
- **GRANDMOTHER** — Grandmother Phantum is an elderly European-descended woman fully at home in Keel, small and frail but still capable, with soft pale-brown skin, wispy silver hair pinned loosely at the nape, cloudy blue-grey eyes and a lined face that can shift abruptly from confusion to piercing clarity. She wears a faded plum cardigan over a cream blouse, a long charcoal skirt, thick socks and soft house shoes; her silhouette is folded inward around a worn chair. Her signature detail is a smal
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde

## Page 17 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 19 (right) — Adrian’s recording makes the inheritance morally complicated.

**Panel 1.** Adrian’s blue projection appears above the workbench.

ADRIAN: Alex. If you’re seeing this, I failed to come back.

**Panel 2.** Adrian looks directly toward Alex.

ADRIAN: I borrowed too much. I gambled some of it. I hurt you and Grandmother.

**Panel 3.** Alex grips the watch.

ADRIAN: The work was shared. The mistakes were mine.

**Panel 4.** The projection flickers.

ADRIAN: Question everything. Especially me.

PAGE CHECK: Adrian admits harm without presenting himself as a flawless inventor.

---
```

## Page 20 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **BI11BOT** — Bi11bot is a rugged, weathered industrial robot with an oversized round head made of segmented, faded industrial-blue panels with visible seams; two large round warm-amber optical eyes recessed in heavy metal rings; side-mounted round ear assemblies; broad rounded shoulder plates; angular chest armor marked with a white “Bi11”; exposed pistons; articulated metal fingers; compact legs and broad feet. His paint is chipped mustard-yellow with a stripe, exposing steel, dark joints,
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is

```text
## Page 20 (left) — Alpha enters as a concrete restriction.

**Panel 1.** Alex’s identity band flashes. The apartment door’s node turns amber.

DISPLAY: `MOVEMENT AUTHORIZATION: TEMPORARILY SUSPENDED.`

**Panel 2.** Alex tries the door. It does not open.

ALEX: Because the band stopped reporting?

BI11BOT: The workshop woke. The system noticed.

**Panel 3.** Bi11bot projects the infrastructure spine connecting Keel, Halyard and Oasis. Water, rail and data lines share the same route.

**Panel 4.** Ada tries the door again.

ADA: It listens to a screen.

BI11BOT: To whoever writes the screen.

PAGE CHECK: Alpha is introduced through a locked door and a distributed system.

---
```

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

## Page 25 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **BI11BOT** — Bi11bot is a rugged, weathered industrial robot with an oversized round head made of segmented, faded industrial-blue panels with visible seams; two large round warm-amber optical eyes recessed in heavy metal rings; side-mounted round ear assemblies; broad rounded shoulder plates; angular chest armor marked with a white “Bi11”; exposed pistons; articulated metal fingers; compact legs and broad feet. His paint is chipped mustard-yellow with a stripe, exposing steel, dark joints,
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **FOREMAN-7** — Foreman-7 is a broad-shouldered human Keel mine foreman in his forties, with dark brown skin, a shaved head, tired dark eyes and a square face mostly obscured by a dust mask and a white-and-amber Alpha-linked supervisory harness. He wears a reinforced charcoal mining coat, plated work trousers, steel-toed boots and a heavy helmet with a small control node at the throat; his silhouette is blocky, official and difficult to read. His signature detail is the white-and-amber node at
- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ

```text
TOMAS: I’d use the door they forgot.

PAGE CHECK: B11’s error occurs before Tomas reveals the route.
```

## Page 27 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ
- **BI11BOT** — Bi11bot is a rugged, weathered industrial robot with an oversized round head made of segmented, faded industrial-blue panels with visible seams; two large round warm-amber optical eyes recessed in heavy metal rings; side-mounted round ear assemblies; broad rounded shoulder plates; angular chest armor marked with a white “Bi11”; exposed pistons; articulated metal fingers; compact legs and broad feet. His paint is chipped mustard-yellow with a stripe, exposing steel, dark joints,
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **FOREMAN-7** — Foreman-7 is a broad-shouldered human Keel mine foreman in his forties, with dark brown skin, a shaved head, tired dark eyes and a square face mostly obscured by a dust mask and a white-and-amber Alpha-linked supervisory harness. He wears a reinforced charcoal mining coat, plated work trousers, steel-toed boots and a heavy helmet with a small control node at the throat; his silhouette is blocky, official and difficult to read. ## Page 30 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **BI11BOT** — Bi11bot is a rugged, weathered industrial robot with an oversized round head made of segmented, faded industrial-blue panels with visible seams; two large round warm-amber optical eyes recessed in heavy metal rings; side-mounted round ear assemblies; broad rounded shoulder plates; angular chest armor marked with a white “Bi11”; exposed pistons; articulated metal fingers; compact legs and broad feet. His paint is chipped mustard-yellow with a stripe, exposing steel, dark joints,
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

## Page 35 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **PILAR** — Pilar Veyra is a sturdy Keel clinic worker in her forties, with medium-brown skin, dark hair braided at the nape, dark attentive eyes and a broad, reassuring face. She wears a faded teal clinic jacket over a charcoal shirt, durable trousers, flat shoes and a paper medication ledger secured in a clear sleeve; her silhouette is practical and steady. Her signature detail is a pencil tucked behind her right ear and a handwritten dose grid on her wrist. **Voice** — Calm, plain and
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

## Page 40 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

He wears a charcoal suit with a platinum collar pin, polished boots and no House crest; his silhouette is long, clean and deliberately neutral, accompanied when necessary by discreet aides rather than visible force. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **DIRECTOR** — Director Cassian Lock is a silver-templed Northern European-descended man in his early sixties, tall and lean with light skin, pale blue-grey eyes, a smooth square jaw and immaculate charcoal hair combed back from a high forehead. He wears a charcoal suit with a platinum collar pin, polished boots and no House crest; his silhouette is long, clean and deliberately neutral, accompanied when necessary by discreet aides rather than visible force.
```text
## Page 40 (left) — Lock offers Alex relief in exchange for control.

**Panel 1.** Director Cassian Lock approaches, examining the registry before Alex.

LOCK: Assign the estate to House administration. Your debt clears. Halyard access becomes permanent.

**Panel 2.** Behind him, three House emblems divide the registry screen: Caldera, Vector and Orbital.

**Panel 3.**

LOCK: Refuse, and immediate commercial rights freeze pending review.

ALEX: You’re asking me to sell work I didn’t make.

LOCK: I’m offering scale, employment and a clean account.

**Panel 4.** Alex closes the registry. Lock taps the display. The estate locks.

ALEX: No.

LOCK: Then find the people who made it.

PAGE CHECK: Lock’s offer is genuine; refusal creates the collaborator search.

---
```

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **LINA** — Dr. Lina Vey is an Indigenous Andean and mestiza woman in her mid-forties, compact and slightly stooped from years at benches, with medium-brown skin, dark hair threaded with silver and tied low, dark almond-shaped eyes and a narrow, alert face. She wears a maintenance-grade water-and-chemistry House uniform under a patched technical coat, flat work shoes and a stained utility belt; her silhouette is practical and worker-shaped despite her authority. Her signature detail is a w
- **REN** — Ren Okafor is a woman in her late thirties with a broad, muscular build, deep brown skin, a shaved head, dark eyes and a blunt, self-possessed face; her ancestry is not inferred from her surname. She wears a patched Orbital House fabrication suit with scorch marks, a heat-shielded apron, heavy work boots and a clipped respirator; her silhouette is broad-shouldered, grounded and visibly built for outer-rim machinery. Her signature detail is a pair of scorched red gloves tucked i

```text
## Page 42 (left) — The group chooses a safe, small objective.

**Panel 1.** In a rented workroom, the four components surround a cloudy Keel water sample.

ALEX: Keel has water now. It can’t use it.

**Panel 2.** Lina tests the sample and rejects a membrane.

LINA: Not that one. It lies under heat.

**Panel 3.** The collaborators connect the remaining components. The systems fail to communicate.

**Panel 4.** The readout drops to `11% SIMULATED EFFICIENCY.`

SFX: CHUFF

REN: Good. Now we know where it breaks.

PAGE CHECK: Failure becomes a practical design problem, not a lesson.

---
```

```text
## Page 43 (right) — The collaborators accept responsibility for a repairable product.

**Panel 1.** Alex cuts a physical sleeve to bridge incompatible standards. B11 watches from a shelf.

BI11BOT: Who carries the downside?

ALEX: The people who sign it.

**Panel 2.** Suri installs a bounded bypass around the living filter.

SURI: If this fails, the unit stops.

**Panel 3.** Lina marks testing limits. Ren adds service panels. Jalen records the failure conditions.

**Panel 4.** A dated work board advances through several weeks: fabrication, testing and installation. The compact purifier runs steadily.

DISPLAY: `91% SIMULATED EFFICIENCY.`  
`FIELD TEST — NOT POTABLE-OUTPUT GUARANTEE.`

PAGE CHECK: Time passes; the proof is not mistaken for a finished product.

---
```

```text
## Page 44 (left) — The contributors retain their rights and make a legitimate first sale.

**Panel 1.** A House review team enters. Alex stands with the four contributors.

ALEX: Lina made the membrane. Suri kept the biofilter alive. Jalen built the routing. Ren made it serviceable.

**Panel 2.** Lock reviews the testing records.

LOCK: House administration would make this easier.

LINA: It would make the names disappear.

**Panel 3.** Lina gives Alex the worn bronze Maker’s Seal. It projects the contributors’ names, testing obligations and warranty reserve.

REN: If it fails, we answer for it.

**Panel 4.** WEEKS LATER. A small Keel shop licenses the tested unit. The display divides 38 credits:

`12 — ALEX DEBT ACCOUNT`  
`18 — CONTRIBUTOR VENTURE`  
`8 — MAINTENANCE / WARRANTY RESERVE`

A House notice appears behind Lock: `COMPETING INDUSTRIAL UNIT IN DEVELOPMENT.`

PAGE CHECK: The first sale creates earned value and a continuing commercial challenge.

---
```

## Page 45 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

```text
## Page 46 (left) — Ada confronts her mother.

**Panel 1.** In a civic archive, Leona Veyra stands among old records. Ada stops several steps away.

LEONA: You’re taller.

ADA: That’s what you have?

**Panel 2.** Leona opens a stone-grey box containing a sage-gold Covenant band.

LEONA: Ada. My daughter.

**Panel 3.** Leona looks at the child’s object inside the box.

LEONA: I knew where you were within a year.

ADA: Then you knew enough to come.

**Panel 4.**

LEONA: I believed you were safer not knowing I was looking.

ADA: Safe for whom?

PAGE CHECK: The mother-daughter relationship is explicit and active.
```

## Page 47 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

She wears a sage-green structured coat over stone-coloured clothing, practical low shoes and a small metal clasp shaped like a closed water drop; her silhouette is formal but not fragile. Her signature detail is a stone-grey box containing her Covenant b
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde
- **MERA** — Mera Vale is a silver-templed white Latin American or Southern European-descended woman in her late fifties, tall and slender with pale olive skin, a precise bob of silver hair, cool grey eyes, a long controlled face and an immaculate upright posture. She wears pearl, graphite, charcoal and platinum administrative clothing with no visible House crest; her silhouette is narrow, vertical and composed. ## Page 47 (right) — Leona opens the vote record that implicates her.
